- **[xPrivo](https://topxai.cn/ai/xprivo)** — 主打隐私的开源 AI 聊天助手，官方称不记录对话、自身也无法访问聊天记录，且不会用于模型训练；聊天内容仅保存在浏览器本地，只使用功能性 Cookie，并提供独立搜索入口。 · [website](https://www.xprivo.com/)
- **[iFlytek Spark](https://topxai.cn/ai/xunfei-xinghuo)** — iFlytek Spark is the company's cognitive large model, covering Q&A, content writing, image generation, slide decks, code, meeting notes, translation and photo-based problem solving, with an API for developers. · [website](https://xinghuo.xfyun.cn/)
- **[Yeschat](https://topxai.cn/ai/yeschat)** — 聚合 GPT、Claude、Gemini 等模型的 AI 对话与智能体平台，可读 PDF、Excel 并联网检索，另提供图像、视频与音乐生成，官方称免费版每天 15 条消息。 · [website](https://www.yeschat.ai)
- **[腾讯元宝](https://topxai.cn/ai/yuanbao)** — 腾讯推出的全能 AI 助手，接入混元 Hy4 preview 模型，支持深度推理、多风格写作、AI 搜索、识图与生图 P 图、拍题答疑，覆盖学习、办公与生活场景，网页与移动端可用。 · [website](https://yuanbao.tencent.com/)
- **[元气AI Bot](https://topxai.cn/ai/yuanqi-bot)** — 北京灵豹智能科技推出的电脑端 AI 助手，官网称一句话指令即可自动完成系统清理、故障修复、软件装卸、批量文件处理与办公提效，并支持手机远程操控电脑，一键安装免费使用。 · [website](https://yuanqiai.net)
- **[Z.ai](https://topxai.cn/ai/z-ai)** — Zhipu's advanced AI chat and agent platform, currently headlined by GLM-5.3-Flash and positioned simply as a fast, intelligent, reliable chatbot and agent. · [website](https://chat.z.ai/)

## AI Note-taking

<sub>[↑ Back to top](#table-of-contents) · `101 tools`</sub>

- **[3MinTop](https://topxai.cn/ai/3mintop)** — 面向读书人的 AI 阅读助手，官方称把复杂书籍拆成三分钟可读的章节要点，内置电子书阅读器、全文翻译、思维导图与内容问答。 · [website](https://3min.top/)

- **[AFFiNE](https://topxai.cn/ai/affine)** — 开源知识库与协作工作区，把文档、白板、数据库和可选 AI 合并在同一画布，采用本地优先架构，官方称编辑器以 MIT 协议授权、可免费自托管。 · [website](https://affine.pro/)

- **[Attention](https://topxai.cn/ai/attention)** — 面向销售团队的 AI 智能体平台，官方称自动沉淀会议与邮件中的信号并起草下一步动作，销售确认后执行，动作全程可审计可追溯。 · [website](https://www.attention.com/)
- **[Audionotes](https://topxai.cn/ai/audionotes)** — 跨端 AI 笔记工具，把语音、音频、视频与文字转成结构化笔记，官方称支持 99 种以上语言、说话人识别与摘要模板，覆盖会议、课程、日记等场景，提供 iOS、Android 与网页端。 · [website](https://www.audionotes.app/)

- **[Avoma](https://topxai.cn/ai/avoma)** — 把会议记录、日程安排、会话分析与营收分析合在一处的 AI 平台，可自动转录并生成摘要、写进 CRM，还能给通话打分做销售陪练，官方称已服务上千家机构。 · [website](https://www.avoma.com/)

- **[Granola](https://topxai.cn/ai/granola)** — AI notepad for people in back-to-back meetings. No meeting bot — it captures calls from your computer's audio and produces enhanced notes and action items on Mac, Windows, iOS and Android. · [website](https://www.granola.ai/)

- **[Jamie](https://topxai.cn/ai/jamie)** — Privacy-first AI meeting note taker that needs no meeting bot, producing structured multilingual notes, transcripts and action items. GDPR compliant and syncs to Notion and others. · [website](https://www.meetjamie.ai/)

- **[Mem](https://topxai.cn/ai/mem)** — AI-agent-centred note workspace: capture notes, meetings, ideas and research without friction, then let it organise them into durable memory. The agent grasps task goals and steps in to push work forward. · [website](https://mem.ai/)

- **[Mowen](https://topxai.cn/ai/mowen)** — Lightweight note-taking and publishing tool built on the idea that recording is creating, available on the web and as a mini program, so notes can be shared, published and monetised. · [website](https://mowen.cn/)
- **[mymind](https://topxai.cn/ai/mymind)** — A private second brain: AI remembers your notes, images, bookmarks and clippings so you never have to sort them into folders, all kept in one private space. · [website](https://mymind.com/)

- **[Aidetectorwriter](https://topxai.cn/ai/aidetectorwriter)** — 免费 AI 内容检测工具，支持批量上传文件、逐句高亮 AI 生成内容并自动生成 PDF 检测报告，官方称模型覆盖全语言，单次可检测 30 万字符文本。 · [website](https://aidetectorwriter.com/)

- **[AI or Not](https://topxai.cn/ai/aiornot)** — AI detection across images, text, music, speech and video, distinguishing AI-generated content from deepfakes with a claimed 98.9% accuracy. Free sandbox, API and usage-based pricing, with on-premise deployment. · [website](https://aiornot.com/)
- **[BypassAI](https://topxai.cn/ai/bypassai)** — 以绕过 AI 检测为卖点的改写工具，官方称能把 AI 文本改写成类人写作，并提供检测、查重与多语言支持，页面标注服务两百万以上用户。 · [website](https://bypassai.ai)

## About

This repository is the open mirror of [topxai.cn](https://topxai.cn), turning the AI tools catalogued on the site into a list that is readable by humans and consumable by programs.

- **Chinese-first**: the [简体中文](./README.md) version is the default.
- **Auto-synced**: a GitHub Action pulls fresh data from the site API every day and regenerates this file.
- **Structured data**: the `data/` folder ships the same content as JSON and CSV.

## Data files

| File | Description |
| --- | --- |
| [`data/tools.json`](./data/tools.json) | Structured data for every tool (bilingual name & description, category, tags, official URL) |
| [`data/categories.json`](./data/categories.json) | Category definitions and grouping |
| [`data/tools.csv`](./data/tools.csv) | The same data as a spreadsheet, easy to open in Excel |

See [CONTRIBUTING.md](./CONTRIBUTING.md) for inclusion criteria.
